| 23:00 UTC | LEAD | F20 OPENED (HIGH, memory safety on every script-graph restore that rebuilds a coroutine — relaunch, resync heal, rollback probes): W124-2's drop3-10 ASan report D:\mx\w124b\asan\report.52732 is a heap-buffer-overflow WRITE 8 at 0 bytes past a coroutine's 384-byte stack in lj_meta.c:127 mmcall (lj_meta_tget under lj_ff_coroutine_resume, thread T72 = the threaded AI pass) on a thread allocated by stack_init in lua_newthread from coroutine.create inside LuaStateWrapper::RestoreScriptGraph <- MovableMan::RestoreScriptGraphs <- RestartActivityCandidate <- HandleControllerReplayFailure. Lead-read cause in Source/Managers/LuaThreadCodec.cpp ThreadRestore (:343): the coroutine's stack is grown by `lua_checkstack(co, top + 16)` — the saved top slot — while LuaJIT's VM relies on base + framesize <= maxstack for every live Lua frame (established at call time, never re-checked on resume); a yielded frame's top is the yield's argument top, far below base + framesize for a function with many locals, so the first register write or metamethod call past the allocation corrupts the heap. W136 SPAWNED (Grok, alias-walk stage2/coroutine-stack-fit from 4dd386fb51, 2.5 h): size by max over Lua frames of index + 1 + framesize (from the saved slots' functions), a post-restore safety net, a _ScriptGraphThreadStackFits query, RED-first script-graph arm with a 96-local frame, suite 11/11. |
